- **diorama-volume**: Create 2.5D assets with `@core/volume` in Diorama — props, scenery pieces, living scenery (doors, chests, levers), equipment parts, effects, item icons, HUD pieces, and anything that must match the project's 3/4 camera or be exported in several facings (directions). - **diorama-worlds**: Build narrative miniature worlds with a fixed orthographic camera, textured terrain, articulated people and coordinated looping actions. Four finishes, miniature, pixel, halftone and blueprint, use the same Three.js scene. This is a separate skill from the diorama SVG illustrations; prefer the diorama skill for lightweight UI illustrations, line art and empty states, and diorama-pixel for dependency-free dithered pixel-art miniatures.
